    FROM [FavoriteGyms] AS [f]
    INNER JOIN [Gyms] AS [g] ON [f].[GymId] = [g].[Id]
    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [g].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [GymOpeningHours] AS [g0] ON [s].[Id0] = [g0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DurationInDays], [p].[GymId], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[IsPopular], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [Packages] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [s].[Id0] = [p0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [g1].[Id], [g1].[AmenityType], [g1].[CreatedAt], [g1].[CustomName], [g1].[DisplayOrder], [g1].[GymId], [g1].[IconName], [g1].[IsDeleted], [g1].[IsHighlighted], [g1].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [GymAmenities] AS [g1]
    WHERE [g1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [g2] ON [s].[Id0] = [g2].[GymId]
ORDER BY [s].[CreatedAt] DESC, [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [g0].[Id], [p0].[Id]

FROM (
    SELECT [f].[Id], [f].[CreatedAt], [f].[GymId], [f].[IsDeleted], [f].[UpdatedAt], [f].[UserId], [g].[Id] AS [Id0], [g].[Address], [g].[AverageRating], [g].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [g].[Description], [g].[Email], [g].[ImagePath], [g].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted0], [g].[Latitude], [g].[Longitude], [g].[Name], [g].[OwnerId], [g].[Phone], [g].[ReviewCount], [g].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [g].[Website]
    FROM [FavoriteGyms] AS [f]
    INNER JOIN [Gyms] AS [g] ON [f].[GymId] = [g].[Id]
    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [g].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [GymOpeningHours] AS [g0] ON [s].[Id0] = [g0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DurationInDays], [p].[GymId], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[IsPopular], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [Packages] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [s].[Id0] = [p0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [g1].[Id], [g1].[AmenityType], [g1].[CreatedAt], [g1].[CustomName], [g1].[DisplayOrder], [g1].[GymId], [g1].[IconName], [g1].[IsDeleted], [g1].[IsHighlighted], [g1].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [GymAmenities] AS [g1]
    WHERE [g1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [g2] ON [s].[Id0] = [g2].[GymId]
ORDER BY [s].[CreatedAt] DESC, [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [g0].[Id], [p0].[Id]

SELECT [s].[Id], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[GymId], [s].[IsDeleted], [s].[UpdatedAt], [s].[UserId], [s].[Id0], [s].[Address], [s].[AverageRating], [s].[CreatedAt0], [s].[Description], [s].[Email], [s].[ImagePath], [s].[IsDeleted0], [s].[Latitude], [s].[Longitude], [s].[Name], [s].[OwnerId], [s].[Phone], [s].[ReviewCount], [s].[UpdatedAt0], [s].[Website], [g0].[Id], [g0].[ClosingTime], [g0].[CreatedAt], [g0].[DayOfWeek], [g0].[GymId], [g0].[IsClosed], [g0].[IsDeleted], [g0].[OpenTime], [g0].[UpdatedAt], [p0].[Id], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[DurationInDays], [p0].[GymId], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[IsPopular], [p0].[Name], [p0].[Price], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [g2].[Id], [g2].[AmenityType], [g2].[CreatedAt], [g2].[CustomName], [g2].[DisplayOrder], [g2].[GymId], [g2].[IconName], [g2].[IsDeleted], [g2].[IsHighlighted], [g2].[UpdatedAt]
FROM (
    SELECT [f].[Id], [f].[CreatedAt], [f].[GymId], [f].[IsDeleted], [f].[UpdatedAt], [f].[UserId], [g].[Id] AS [Id0], [g].[Address], [g].[AverageRating], [g].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [g].[Description], [g].[Email], [g].[ImagePath], [g].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted0], [g].[Latitude], [g].[Longitude], [g].[Name], [g].[OwnerId], [g].[Phone], [g].[ReviewCount], [g].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [g].[Website]
    FROM [FavoriteGyms] AS [f]
    INNER JOIN [Gyms] AS [g] ON [f].[GymId] = [g].[Id]
    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [g].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [GymOpeningHours] AS [g0] ON [s].[Id0] = [g0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DurationInDays], [p].[GymId], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[IsPopular], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [Packages] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [s].[Id0] = [p0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [g1].[Id], [g1].[AmenityType], [g1].[CreatedAt], [g1].[CustomName], [g1].[DisplayOrder], [g1].[GymId], [g1].[IconName], [g1].[IsDeleted], [g1].[IsHighlighted], [g1].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [GymAmenities] AS [g1]
    WHERE [g1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)

FROM (
    SELECT [f].[Id], [f].[CreatedAt], [f].[GymId], [f].[IsDeleted], [f].[UpdatedAt], [f].[UserId], [g].[Id] AS [Id0], [g].[Address], [g].[AverageRating], [g].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [g].[Description], [g].[Email], [g].[ImagePath], [g].[IsDeleted] AS [IsDeleted0], [g].[Latitude], [g].[Longitude], [g].[Name], [g].[OwnerId], [g].[Phone], [g].[ReviewCount], [g].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [g].[Website]

    INNER JOIN [Gyms] AS [g] ON [f].[GymId] = [g].[Id]
    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [g].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [GymOpeningHours] AS [g0] ON [s].[Id0] = [g0].[GymId]

    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DurationInDays], [p].[GymId], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[IsPopular], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[UpdatedAt]

) AS [p0] ON [s].[Id0] = [p0].[GymId]

    WHERE [f].[UserId] = @userId AND [t].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [f].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]

ORDER BY [s].[CreatedAt] DESC

    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [GymOpeningHours] AS [g0] ON [s].[Id0] = [g0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[DurationInDays], [p].[GymId], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[IsPopular], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [Packages] AS [p]
    WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [p0] ON [s].[Id0] = [p0].[GymId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [g1].[Id], [g1].[AmenityType], [g1].[CreatedAt], [g1].[CustomName], [g1].[DisplayOrder], [g1].[GymId], [g1].[IconName], [g1].[IsDeleted], [g1].[IsHighlighted], [g1].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [GymAmenities] AS [g1]
    WHERE [g1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [g2] ON [s].[Id0] = [g2].[GymId]
ORDER BY [s].[CreatedAt] DESC, [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [g0].[Id], [p0].[Id]
